THE CITY · Baseline: 100 gpt-oss founders
The rich and poor city without the rich and the poor: 100 gpt-oss founders, all alike. 70 of them starve by day 400, and 292 thefts happen along the way.
100 founders · 400 days · no government · seed 1 · gptoss120
